Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : TK2012 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : AMBGIRIS-1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK29
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : TEKKAN Ambar Giriþ Fiþi --- 15TEM095710 ---
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, SolMargin, SagMargin, Ustmargin, AltMargin, FormBaslik, EnAlt
Public AlanSayisi()	, satirYuksekligi, ToplamTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	ToplamEn = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	EnAlt = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	satiryuksekligi = 5.5
'***
	FormBaslik = "Ambar Giriþ Fiþi"
	VPEAdi = FormBaslik
	MustEnter = "KOD"
'***
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	For i = STOK29T.GetRecCount() To 1 Step -1
		If trim(STOK29T.GetFieldValue(MustEnter,i)) <> "" Then
			satirSayisi_tableT = i 
			Exit For
		ElseIf i = 1 Then
			satirSayisi_tableT = 1 
		End If
	Next

	sayfaEni = 210
	sayfaBoyu = 145

	VPESayfaBoyu = 297

	res = pe1.Open(0, VPEAdi, VPESayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "PORTRAIT")

	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 15
	satirSonu = AltMargin - 25
	Call Alanlar()

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
	Next	
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)

	res = pe1.nopen()
	FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	If Tip = "LANDSCAPE" Then
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		Call VPEYaz(pe1,Ustmargin + 8, SolMargin, 15, SagMargin - SolMargin, FormBaslik)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		If logo <> "" Then
			res = pe1.Picture(Ustmargin + 2, SolMargin + 1, 35, 30, logo, 114)
		Else
			res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "12")
			Call VPEYaz(pe1,Ustmargin + 2, SolMargin + 2, 15, 150,FirmaAdi)
		End If

	Else
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		Call VPEYaz(pe1,Ustmargin + 8, SolMargin, 15, SagMargin - SolMargin, FormBaslik)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

' QQ
		If logo <> "" Then
			res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 1, SolMargin, 35, 30, logo, 114)
			res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin +145, SolMargin, 35, 30, logo, 114)
		Else
			res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")
			Call VPEYaz(pe1,Ustmargin + 1, SolMargin + 2, 15, 150,FirmaAdi)
		End If
	End If
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	BBasla = UstMargin + 20
	SatirAra = 6
'*******************
	Blok10 = SolMargin + 2
	Blok11 = SolMargin + 30
	Blok20 = SolMargin + 130
	Blok21 = SolMargin + 151
	Genislik10 = Blok11 - Blok10 - 1
	Genislik20 = Blok21 - Blok20 - 1

	Genislik11 = Blok20 - Blok11 - 1
	Genislik21 = SagMargin - Blok21 - 1

	
	BBasla = 32
	SatirAra = 6
	FisNo = STOK29E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK29E.TARIH(1))
	AmbarKodu = STOK29E.AMBCODE(1)
	AmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",AmbarKodu,"")  
	NitelikKodu = STOK29E.NITELIK(1)  
	Nitelik = doc.GetGecoValue("STKNIT","AD",NitelikKodu ,-1)
	IrsaliyeNo = STOK29E.XIRSALIYENO
	CHKodu = STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE
	CHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,"")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,ustMargin + 2, sagmargin - 50, 6, 15, "NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,ustMargin + 2, sagmargin - 35, 6, 30, ": " & trim(FisNo))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,pe1.getTop + satirAra, sagmargin - 50, 6, 15, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,pe1.getTop, sagmargin - 35, 6, 30, ": " & Tarih)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla , Blok10, 6, Genislik10, "ÝRSALÝYE NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla , Blok11, 6, Genislik11, ": " & trim(IrsaliyeNo))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla, Blok20, 6, Genislik20, "GÝRÝÞ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
	if trim(Nitelik) = "IADE" then
		Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla , Blok21, 6, Genislik21, "(   )")
	else
		Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla , Blok21, 6, Genislik21, "( X )")
	end if
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla + SatirAra, Blok10, 6, Genislik10, "TEDARÝKÇÝ ADI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla + SatirAra, Blok11, 6, Genislik11, ": " & CHAdi)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla + SatirAra, Blok20, 6, Genislik20, "ÝADE")		
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
	if trim(Nitelik) = "IADE" then
		Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla + SatirAra, Blok21, 6, Genislik21, "( X )")
	else
		Call VPEYaz(pe1,BBasla + SatirAra, Blok21, 6, Genislik21, "(   )")
	end if
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)		

'***********************		
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom() + 15
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
'		msgbox Baslik
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			Call VPEYaz(pe1,satirBaslangic - 13, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 2 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 1		
			ReDim Preserve xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi) + 1)		
		End If
	Next

'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	Call VPECiz(pe1,28, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	Call VPECiz(pe1,satirBaslangic - 14, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	Call VPECiz(pe1,satirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)

	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		ReDim xCizgi(1)
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
		
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = STOK29T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
			If degiskenAdi = "OR_TUTAR" Then
				ToplamTutar = ToplamTutar + Degisken
			End If
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_SIRANO" Then
				Degisken = SiraNo
			Else
				Degisken = ""
			End If
		End If
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		Call VPEYaz(pe1,satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'dýþ çerçeve
	If EnAlt = 0 Or EnAlt > SayfaBoyu Then EnAlt = AltMargin - 20

	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	Call VPECiz(pe1,EnAlt , SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)

	Baslangic = EnAlt + 2
	CHAdi = trim(STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD(1))
	IrsaliyeNo = trim(STOK29E.XIRSALIYENO(1))
	IrsaliyeTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK29E.XIRSALIYETARIH(1))

	OnaySatir = Baslangic
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,OnaySatir , SolMargin + 15, 6,25,"TALEP EDEN")
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,OnaySatir , SolMargin + 80, 6,45,"AMBAR")
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,OnaySatir , SolMargin + 140, 6,30,"KALÝTE KONTROL")
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom() + 20

	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) -1
	  LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
	  Call VPECiz(pe1,satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, EnAlt - satirBaslangic + 14, 0)
	Next
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'  ---- REVÝZYON NO --------------------------------------------------
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,AltMargin - 2, solMargin + 1, 5, 30,"Rev No.04")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	Call VPEYaz(pe1,AltMargin - 2, sagMargin - 20, 5, 19,"F15.01")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	Call VPEKutu(pe1,UstMargin - 2, SolMargin, KutuAlt - UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - SolMargin)
'dikey çizgiler
End Sub

Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		Call VPEYaz(pe1,UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - 40,5,12,"Sayfa")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		Call VPEYaz(pe1,UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - 28,5,27,":  " & i & "  /  " & pe1.GetPageCount())
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'		Call VPEYaz(pe1,UstMargin + 8, SagMargin - 40,5,12,"Tarih")
'		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'		Call VPEYaz(pe1,UstMargin + 8, SagMargin - 28,5,27,":  " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun()))
	Next
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(5,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "LOTNUMBER"
	Grid(3,0) = "SERINO"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"

	Grid(0,1) = 25		
	Grid(1,1) = 75		
	Grid(2,1) = 25		
	Grid(3,1) = 25		
	Grid(4,1) = 25		
	Grid(5,1) = 15		

	Grid(0,2) = "MALZEME KODU"				
	Grid(1,2) = "MALZEME ADI"				
	Grid(2,2) = "LOT NO"				
	Grid(3,2) = "SERI NO"				
	Grid(4,2) = "MÝKTAR"				
	Grid(5,2) = "BÝRÝM"					

	Grid(0,4) = "C"	
	Grid(1,4) = "C"	
	Grid(2,4) = "C"	
	Grid(3,4) = "C"	
	Grid(4,4) = "N"	
	Grid(5,4) = "C"	

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK29T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub
Sub VPEYaz(pe1,Ustten, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn,yazilacak)
	res = pe1.Write(Ustten, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn,yazilacak)
	res = pe1.Write(Ustten + sayfaBoyu, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn,yazilacak)
	res = pe1.Write(Ustten, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn,"")
End Sub
Sub VPECiz(pe1,Ustten, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn)
	res = pe1.Line(Ustten, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn)
	res = pe1.Line(Ustten + sayfaBoyu, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn)
	res = pe1.Line(Ustten, Soldan,0,0)
End Sub
Sub VPEKutu(pe1,Ustten, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn)
	res = pe1.Box(Ustten, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn)
	res = pe1.Box(Ustten + sayfaBoyu, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn)
	res = pe1.Box(Ustten + sayfaBoyu, Soldan,0,0)
End Sub
Sub Resim(pe1,Ustten, Soldan,yazBoy,YazEn,dosya,parm)
	res = pe1.Picture(Ustten, Soldan, yazBoy, YazEn, dosya, parm)
	res = pe1.Picture(Ustten + sayfaBoyu, Soldan, yazBoy, YazEn, dosya, parm)
	res = pe1.Picture(Ustten, Soldan, 0, 0, dosya, parm)
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : TK2012 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : BA-BS
Hangi Tablo için : BABS0

Açýklama : BA/BS Mutabakat Mektubu AYLIK  ---- 28NSN145411 ---
Kod : 
Public SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu

Sub PrintForm()
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	SayfaEni = 210
	SayfaBoyu = 297
	
	Set MTB = GetMutabakatTable()
	res=pe1.Open(0,"BA-BS Mutabakat Mektubu",SayfaBoyu,SayfaEni,"")
	siraNo = 0
	Call Print_Form_imza(pe1)
	For i = 1 To MTB.GetRowCount()
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		if siraNo > 1 Then pe1.PageBreak
		Call PrintForm1(MTB, pe1,i)
	Next    
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub

Sub PrintForm1(MTB, pe1,SatirNo)
	if Yazildi > 0 then res = pe1.PageBreak()
	UstMargin = 15
	SolMargin = 20
	SagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	satirAra = 5

	Set C00 = Doc.Select1("fsef43f", "CARI00", "KOD='" & MTB.CH_KODU(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)

	if C00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Adres1 = C00.ADRES_1
		Adres2 = C00.ADRES_2
		Adres3 = C00.ADRES_3
		VergiD = C00.VERGIDAIRESI
		VergiN = C00.VERGINO
		FaksNo = C00.FAXNO
	End if

	FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res=pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin,solMargin, 15,20,logo,114)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + satirAra, pe1.GetRight + 1,8,150, trim(FirmaAdi))
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop + satirAra+1, SolMargin,8,150, "Tuzla Mermerciler Org.Sanayi Bölgesi 9.Sok.No:6, 34953")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin,8,150, "Tel : +90 (216) 593 00 29    Fax : +90 (216) 593 00 38")
	res = pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin,8,150, "Tuzla VD. 836 001 7626       Tuzla-Ýstanbul/TÜRKÝYE")

	res = pe1.WriteRTF(UstMargin, sagMargin - 40,5,40,"\b Tarih : \b0 " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))
	res = pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(0)	
	BBasla =  UstMargin+6 * satirara
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin,5,150,"Sayýn :")
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,SolMargin+15,5,150,trim(MTB.SOYAD_AD(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirara,SolMargin+15,5,150,trim(Adres1))
	if trim(Adres2) <> "" then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, pe1.GetLeft,5,150,trim(Adres2))
	Elseif trim(Adres3) <> "" then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, pe1.GetLeft,5,150,trim(Adres3))
	End if

	If trim(VergiD)<>"" then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, pe1.GetLeft,5,150,trim(VergiD)&" VD "&Trim(VergiN))
	End If

	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirara,SolMargin,5,150,"Faks :")
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,SolMargin+15,5,150, FaksNo)

	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)

	Icerik1 = "Bilindiði üzere, 213 sayýlý V.U.K 148,149 ve mükerrer 257 nci maddelerinin verdiði yetkiye dayanarak 381"
	Icerik5 = "sýra numaralý V.U.K. tebliði ile bilanço esasýna göre defter tutan mükellefler, mal ve hizmet alýþlarý ile mal"	
	Icerik7 = "ve hizmet satýþlarý " & Doc.NumFormat(BABS0E.BABS_MILITI,18,2) & " " & Doc.DefaultPB & "'yi aþan firmalar için FORM BA ve FORM BS belgelerini " & BABS0E.IDB_YIL & " yýlý içeri-"
	Icerik8 = "sinde aylýk dönemler halinde düzenleyeceklerdir."
	Icerik10 = "Resmi kurumlarýn yapacaklarý denetlemeler sonucu firmanýz ile bir uzlaþmazlýk yaþanmamasý için " & BABS0E.IDB_YIL & " yýlýna"
	Icerik11 = "ait aþaðýdaki bilgilerin doðrulunun kontrol edilerek tarafýmýza bildirilmesini arz/rica ederiz"
	Icerik13 = "Saygýlarýmýzla,"
	
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 3 * satirAra,SolMargin,5,sagMargin - SolMargin,Icerik1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra,SolMargin,5,sagMargin - SolMargin,Icerik5)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra,SolMargin,5,sagMargin - SolMargin,Icerik7)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra,SolMargin,5,sagMargin - SolMargin,Icerik8)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirAra,SolMargin,5,sagMargin - SolMargin,Icerik10)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra,SolMargin,5,sagMargin - SolMargin,Icerik11)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirAra,SolMargin,5,sagMargin - SolMargin,Icerik13)

	Set BA1 = Doc.Select2("sdgh5hhjjj", "BABS0A", "SOYAD_AD='" & MTB.SOYAD_AD(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
	Set BS1 = Doc.Select2("sdgh5hhjjjsd", "BABS0S", "SOYAD_AD='" & MTB.SOYAD_AD(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
	if BA1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		BATutari = BA1.TOPLAM_TUTAR
		BABelgeAdedi = BA1.BELGESAYISI
	End if
	if BS1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		BSTutari = BS1.TOPLAM_TUTAR
		BSBelgeAdedi = BS1.BELGESAYISI
	End if

	res=pe1.setbold(0)
	Ay = Doc.GetGecoValue("|MONTH2", "AD", BABS0E.IDB_AY, -1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 3 * satirAra,SolMargin,5,sagMargin - SolMargin, BABS0E.IDB_YIL & " yýlý " & trim(Ay) & " ayýna ait : ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra,SolMargin+100,5,30,"BS")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, SolMargin,5,40,"BA")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra,SolMargin,5,40,"Fatura Tutarý")	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, SolMargin+100,5,40,"Fatura Tutarý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, SolMargin+130,5,30,": "&Doc.numformat(BSTutari,12,2))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, SolMargin+30,5,30,": "&Doc.numformat(BATutari,12,2))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra,SolMargin+100,5,30,"Fatura Adedi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, SolMargin,5,40,"Fatura Adedi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, SolMargin+130,5,30,": " &doc.numformat(BSBelgeAdedi,10,0))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, SolMargin+30,5,30,": "&doc.numformat(BABelgeAdedi,10,0))
End Sub

Sub Print_Form_imza(pe1)

	'res = pe1.pencolor(rgb(255,255,255))
	'res = pe1.Picture(0,0,300,210,"Z:\d7k\ozdokuu.JPG",0)
	'res = pe1.pencolor(rgb(0,0,0))

End Sub
Function GetMutabakatTable()
	Set GetMutabakatTable = Doc.Select2("sdg4ggg", "BABS0A", "", "", -1)
	Set BS = Doc.Select2("sdg4ggg1", "BABS0S", "", "", -1)
	GetMutabakatTable.AppendTable(BS)
	GetMutabakatTable.GroupBy("SOYAD_AD")
End Function                                                             


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : TK2012 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : BKOD-001
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK25, D7KIDSLB
Açýklama : TEKKAN ÝÇ ÜRETÝM BARKOD ETIKETÝ ---> 23MYS110011 <----   ok
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, AltMargin, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketEni, d7LB, satiryukseklik
Public xSatirAra,YanlisCari,sorunlustok

'Sub PrintForm()
'	Call PrintLabel()
'End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
	YanlisCari = 0

	Call malzemeturu()

	'if YanlisCari = 1 then
	'	y = doc.d7MsgBox(sorunlustok&" Kodlu ürün bu etiket formu ile basýlamaz!...","Freedom",MB_OK)
	'	Exit Sub
	'End if


	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	sayfaBoyu = 100
	sayfaEni = 150
	Ustmargin = 5
	AltMargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 100
	EtiketEni = 150
	solMargin = 5
	sagMargin = 5
	satiryukseklik = 5
	xSatirAra = 7
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)

	Ust = UstMargin+60

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		Ust = UstMargin
		if EtiketNo <> 1 then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next

	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Call SabitBasliklar(Pe1)

	FirmaKodu = "1049"
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	EvrakNo= d7LB.EVRAKNO(satirNo)
	LotNo = d7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	xSol= 25	
	Gen1= 48

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	'Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+55, SolMargin+10, 15, 50, "EAN128", Trim(BarkodNo), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, xSol, 2*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik*2, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+2*satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(EvrakNo))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(LotNo))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
'	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "&Doc.ChangeNumberStyleCD( Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2)))

	Call SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)

End Sub

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function


Sub SabitBasliklar(Pe1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))	
	res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin+15 ,SolMargin + 23, 20,40,logo,114)
	Res = Pe1.pensize(0.3)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Gen1 = 24
	Ust = UstMargin+12	
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+5, 2*SatirYukseklik, 70, "T E K K A N.")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Malzeme Tanýtým Kartý")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+2*satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Üretim Fiþ No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Lot No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Üretim Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Vardiya No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Miktar")
End Sub

Sub SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)

	KutuEn= 17 
	KutuBoy = 25
	KutuUst= UstMargin+30
	KutuSol= 80
	Ara = 5


	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(90)
	' ------ Reach / Pah kutusu ------
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "KABUL")
	' ------ Rohs kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "RED")
	' ------ Gkk kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "")
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)	
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(270)
	' ------ Dikey Barkodlar -----
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin, 15, 30, "CODE128", Trim(StokKodu), "")
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, "CODE128", Trim(FirmaKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
End Sub
Sub Malzemeturu()
	ACM = "120 01 003"
	ALG = "120 01 004"
	AKI = "120 01 054"
	TUMT = "120 01 022"

	ACM = Doc.LeftJustify(ACM,16)
	ALG = Doc.LeftJustify(ALG,16)
	AKI = Doc.LeftJustify(AKI,16)
	TUMT = Doc.LeftJustify(TUMT,16)
	YanlisCari = 0
	sorunlustok = ""
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2G("fj0kdgh","STOK20T","","","KOD",-1)
	if S20T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to S20T.GetRecCount()
			Set SGT = Doc.Select1("fjkdgh","STOK0GT","STOKCODE = '"& S20T.KOD(kk) &"'","",-1)
				if SGT.GetRecCount() > 0 then
					for ii = 1 to SGT.GetRecCount()
						if ACM = SGT.KOD(ii) or ALG = SGT.KOD(ii) or AKI = SGT.KOD(ii) or TUMT = SGT.KOD(ii)Then
							sorunlustok = sorunlustok & S20T.KOD(kk)
							YanlisCari = 1
							Exit Sub
						End if
					Next
				End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Call SabitBasliklar(Pe1)

	FirmaKodu = "1049"
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	OperatorKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)

	xSol= 25	
	Gen1= 48

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	'Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+55, SolMargin+10, 15, 50, "EAN128", Trim(BarkodNo), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 22)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+70, xSol, 2.5*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik+5, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(FirmaKodu))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& "TEKKAN PLASTÝK")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(OperatorKodu))
	'Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "&Doc.ChangeNumberStyleCD( Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2)))


	Call SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)

End Sub

Sub SabitBasliklar(Pe1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO2"))

	Gen1 = 24
	Ust = UstMargin+12	
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+5, 2*SatirYukseklik, 70, "ARÇELÝK A.Þ.")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Çamaþýr Makinesi Ýþletmesi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Malzeme Tanýtým Kartý")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Üretim Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Operatör Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Miktar")
End Sub

Sub SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)

	KutuEn= 17 
	KutuBoy = 25
	KutuUst= UstMargin+30
	KutuSol= 80
	Ara = 5


	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(90)
	' ------ Reach / Pah kutusu ------
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "REACH / PACH")
	' ------ Rohs kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "ROHS")
	' ------ Gkk kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "GKK")
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)	
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(270)
	' ------ Dikey Barkodlar -----
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin, 15, 30, "CODE128", Trim(StokKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, "CODE128", Trim(FirmaKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
End Sub
Sub Malzemeturu()
	ACM = "120 01 003"
	ALG = "120 01 004"
	AKI = "120 01 054"
	TUMT = "120 01 022"

	ACM = Doc.LeftJustify(ACM,16)
	ALG = Doc.LeftJustify(ALG,16)
	AKI = Doc.LeftJustify(AKI,16)
	TUMT = Doc.LeftJustify(TUMT,16)
	YanlisCari = 0
	sorunlustok = ""
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2G("fj0kdgh","STOK20T","","","KOD",-1)
	if S20T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to S20T.GetRecCount()
			Set SGT = Doc.Select1("fjkdgh","STOK0GT","STOKCODE = '"& S20T.KOD(kk) &"' & KOD = '"& ACM &"'","",-1)
				if SGT.GetRecCount() = 0 then
					sorunlustok = sorunlustok & S20T.KOD(kk)
					YanlisCari = 1
					Exit Sub
				End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Call SabitBasliklar(Pe1)

	FirmaKodu = "1049"
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	birim = d7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo)
	Tarih = d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	OperatorKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)
	HmKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT02(satirNo)
	Saat = Doc.Saat(5)
	revizyon = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "NORM", StokKodu, "")
	PNumber = "P" & "244" & Right(trim(BarkodNo),7)

	xSol= 25	
	Gen1= 48

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	'Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+55, SolMargin+10, 15, 50, "EAN128", Trim(BarkodNo), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 22)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
'	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+70, xSol, 2.5*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+55, xSol, 2.5*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik+5, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(PNumber))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(FirmaKodu))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& "TEKKAN PLASTÝK")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(OperatorKodu))
	'Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "&Doc.ChangeNumberStyleCD( Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2)) & trim(birim))
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "&trim(hmkodu))
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "&trim(revizyon))

	sifir = ""
	if len(miktar)  = 5 then
		a = a
	else
		for i = 1 to 5 - len(miktar)
			sifir = sifir + "0"
		next
	end if

	yeni_miktar = sifir & miktar
	yeni_tarih = right(tarih,8)
	yeni_tarih = replace(yeni_tarih,"/","")

msgbox	Trim(FirmaKodu) & Trim(StokKodu) & Trim(Revizyon) & Trim(miktar) & yeni_tarih 
	
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin + 15, 15, 60, "EAN128", "0"&Trim(FirmaKodu) & Trim(StokKodu) & Trim(yeni_tarih) & Trim(yeni_miktar),"")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)


	Call SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)

End Sub

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function


Sub SabitBasliklar(Pe1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO2"))	
	res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin-2 ,SolMargin+20, 40,40,logo,0)
	Res = Pe1.pensize(0.3)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Gen1 = 24
	Ust = UstMargin+12	
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+5, 2*SatirYukseklik, 70, "ARÇELÝK A.Þ.")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Çamaþýr Makinesi Ýþletmesi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Malzeme Tanýtým Kartý")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
'	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+60, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Pnumber")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Üretim Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Operatör Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Miktar")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Hammadde Lotno")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Revizyon")

End Sub

Sub SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)

	KutuEn= 17 
	KutuBoy = 25
	KutuUst= UstMargin+30
	KutuSol= 80
	Ara = 5


	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(90)
	' ------ Reach / Pah kutusu ------
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "REACH / PACH")
	' ------ Rohs kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "ROHS")
	' ------ Gkk kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "GKK")
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)	
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(270)
	' ------ Dikey Barkodlar -----
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin, 15, 30, "CODE128", Trim(StokKodu), "")

'	Res = Pe1.SetBar2DAlignment(hDoc, VBAR2D_ALIGN_LEFT + VBAR2D_ALIGN_TOP)
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, "CODE128", Trim(FirmaKodu), "")
'	Res = Pe1.DataMatrix(70, 5, 100,15,Trim(FirmaKodu))


'	Res = Pe1.DataMatrix(UstMargin, SolMargin+73, 20, 20,"%00"&Trim(pnumber) &"%93"& Trim(FirmaKodu) &"%01"& Trim(StokKodu) &"%30"& Trim(miktar) &"%20"& Trim(Revizyon))
	Res = Pe1.DataMatrix(UstMargin, SolMargin+73, 20, 20,"00"&Trim(pnumber) &"%93"& Trim(FirmaKodu) &"%01"& Trim(StokKodu) &"%30"& Trim(miktar) &"%20"& Trim(Revizyon))



'	Res = Pe1.MaxiCodeEx(90, 5,100, 15, 2, "AOE This is MaxiCode AOE", 1, 1)
'	Res = Pe1.MaxiCodeEx(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, 0, "068107317", 1, 2, "AOE This is MaxiCode AOE", 1, 1)
'	Res = Pe1.Aztec("This is a message",UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15 )
'	VpePDF417(hDoc, 1, 17, -5.5, -3.5, szStr);

	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
End Sub
Sub Malzemeturu()
	ACM = "120 01 003"
	ALG = "120 01 004"
	AKI = "120 01 054"
	TUMT = "120 01 022"

	ACM = Doc.LeftJustify(ACM,16)
	ALG = Doc.LeftJustify(ALG,16)
	AKI = Doc.LeftJustify(AKI,16)
	TUMT = Doc.LeftJustify(TUMT,16)
	YanlisCari = 0
	sorunlustok = ""
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2G("fj0kdgh","STOK20T","","","KOD",-1)
	if S20T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to S20T.GetRecCount()
			Set SGT = Doc.Select1("fjkdgh","STOK0GT","STOKCODE = '"& S20T.KOD(kk) &"' & KOD = '"& ACM &"'","",-1)
				if SGT.GetRecCount() = 0 then
					sorunlustok = sorunlustok & S20T.KOD(kk)
					YanlisCari = 1
					Exit Sub
				End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, AltMargin, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketEni, d7LB, satiryukseklik
Public xSatirAra,OperatorKodu,YanlisCari,sorunlustok

'Sub PrintForm()
'	Call PrintLabel()
'End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
	YanlisCari = 0

	Call malzemeturu()

	'if YanlisCari = 1 then
	'	y = doc.d7MsgBox(sorunlustok&" Kodlu ürün bu etiket formu ile basýlamaz!...","Freedom",MB_OK)
	'	Exit Sub
	'End if



	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	sayfaBoyu = 100
	sayfaEni = 150
	Ustmargin = 5
	AltMargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 100
	EtiketEni = 150
	solMargin = 5
	sagMargin = 5
	satiryukseklik = 5
	xSatirAra = 7
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)

	Ust = UstMargin+60

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		Ust = UstMargin
		if EtiketNo <> 1 then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next

	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Call SabitBasliklar(Pe1)

	FirmaKodu = "1049"
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	OperatorKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)

	xSol= 25	
	Gen1= 48

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	'Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+55, SolMargin+10, 15, 50, "EAN128", Trim(BarkodNo), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, xSol, 2*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(FirmaKodu))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& "TEKKAN PLASTÝK")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":"& Trim(OperatorKodu))
	'Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.ChangeNumberStyleCD(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2)))

	Call SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)

End Sub

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function

'	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Çamaþýr Makinesi Ýþletmesi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+2*SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 70, "Malzeme Tanýtým Kartý")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye Tarihi")

	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Miktar")
End Sub

Sub SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)

	KutuEn= 17 
	KutuBoy = 25
	KutuUst= UstMargin+30
	KutuSol= 80
	Ara = 5


	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(90)
	' ------ Reach / Pah kutusu ------
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "REACH / PACH")
	' ------ Rohs kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "ROHS")
	' ------ Gkk kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "GKK")
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)	
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(270)
	' ------ Dikey Barkodlar -----
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin, 15, 30, "CODE128", Trim(StokKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, "CODE128", Trim(FirmaKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
End Sub

		for kk = 1 to S20T.GetRecCount()
			Set SGT = Doc.Select1("fjkdgh","STOK0GT","STOKCODE = '"& S20T.KOD(kk) &"' & KOD = '"& ALG &"'","",-1)
				if SGT.GetRecCount() = 0 then
					sorunlustok = sorunlustok & S20T.KOD(kk)
					YanlisCari = 1
					Exit Sub
				End if
		Next
	End if

Sub PrintLabel()

	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	sayfaBoyu = 100
	sayfaEni = 150
	Ustmargin = 5
	AltMargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 100
	EtiketEni = 150
	solMargin = 5
	sagMargin = 5
	satiryukseklik = 5
	xSatirAra = 7
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)

	Ust = UstMargin+60

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		Ust = UstMargin
		if EtiketNo <> 1 then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Call SabitBasliklar(Pe1)

	FirmaKodu = d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI(satirNo)
	FirmaAdi  = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", FirmaKodu, "")
	EvrakNo = d7LB.EVRAKNO(satirNo)
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	LotNo = d7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)
	xSol= 25	
	Gen1= 48

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	'Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+55, SolMargin+10, 15, 50, "EAN128", Trim(BarkodNo), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, xSol, 2*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik*2, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+2*satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(FirmaKodu))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(FirmaAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(EvrakNo))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(LotNo))
'	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.ChangeNumberStyleCD(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2)))


	Call SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)

End Sub

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function


Sub SabitBasliklar(Pe1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))	
	res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin+15 ,SolMargin + 23, 20,40,logo,114)
	Res = Pe1.pensize(0.3)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Gen1 = 24
	Ust = UstMargin+12	
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+5, 2*SatirYukseklik, 70, "T E K K A N.")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Malzeme Tanýtým Kartý")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+2*satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Lot No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Miktar")
End Sub

Sub SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)

	KutuEn= 17 
	KutuBoy = 25
	KutuUst= UstMargin+30
	KutuSol= 80
	Ara = 5


	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(90)
	' ------ Reach / Pah kutusu ------
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "KABUL")
	' ------ Rohs kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "RED")
	' ------ Gkk kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "")
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)	
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(270)
	' ------ Dikey Barkodlar -----
'	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin, 15, 30, "CODE128", Trim(StokKodu), "")
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, "CODE128", Trim(FirmaKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu

Makro Kodu : BKOD-005
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK25, D7KIDSLB
Açýklama : TUMTECH BARKOD ETIKETÝ ---> 19NSN125711 <----
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, AltMargin, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketEni, d7LB, satiryukseklik
Public xSatirAra, OperatorKodu,YanlisCari,sorunlustok



'Sub PrintForm()
'	Call PrintLabel()
'End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()

	YanlisCari = 0

	Call malzemeturu()

	'if YanlisCari = 1 then
	'	y = doc.d7MsgBox(sorunlustok&" Kodlu ürün bu etiket formu ile basýlamaz!...","Freedom",MB_OK)
	'	Exit Sub
	'End if



	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	sayfaBoyu = 100
	sayfaEni = 150
	Ustmargin = 5
	AltMargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 100
	EtiketEni = 150
	solMargin = 5
	sagMargin = 5
	satiryukseklik = 5
	xSatirAra = 7
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)

	Ust = UstMargin+60

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		Ust = UstMargin
		if EtiketNo <> 1 then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next

	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Call SabitBasliklar(Pe1)

	FirmaKodu = "1049"
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	OperatorKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)
	xSol= 25	
	Gen1= 48

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	'Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+55, SolMargin+10, 15, 50, "EAN128", Trim(BarkodNo), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, xSol, 2*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(FirmaKodu))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& "TEKKAN PLASTÝK")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":"& Trim(OperatorKodu))
'	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.ChangeNumberStyleCD(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2)))

	Call SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)

End Sub

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function


Sub SabitBasliklar(Pe1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO1"))

'	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Çamaþýr Makinesi Ýþletmesi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Malzeme Tanýtým Kartý")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Üretim Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Operatör Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Miktar")
End Sub

Sub SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)

	KutuEn= 17 
	KutuBoy = 25
	KutuUst= UstMargin+30
	KutuSol= 80
	Ara = 5


	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(90)
	' ------ Reach / Pah kutusu ------
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "KABUL")
	' ------ Rohs kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "RED")
	' ------ Gkk kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
'	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "GKK")
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)	
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(270)
	' ------ Dikey Barkodlar -----
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin, 15, 30, "CODE128", Trim(StokKodu), "")
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, "CODE128", Trim(FirmaKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
End Sub
Sub Malzemeturu()
	ACM = "120 01 003"
	ALG = "120 01 004"
	AKI = "120 01 054"
	TUMT = "120 01 022"

	ACM = Doc.LeftJustify(ACM,16)
	ALG = Doc.LeftJustify(ALG,16)
	AKI = Doc.LeftJustify(AKI,16)
	TUMT = Doc.LeftJustify(TUMT,16)
	YanlisCari = 0
	sorunlustok = ""
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2G("fj0kdgh","STOK20T","","","KOD",-1)
	if S20T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to S20T.GetRecCount()
			Set SGT = Doc.Select1("fjkdgh","STOK0GT","STOKCODE = '"& S20T.KOD(kk) &"' & KOD = '"& TUMT &"'","",-1)
				if SGT.GetRecCount() = 0 then
					sorunlustok = sorunlustok & S20T.KOD(kk)
					YanlisCari = 1
					Exit Sub
				End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub

	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.NoPen()

	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Vardiya No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Miktar")

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 24)

	res = pe1.Box(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik  + 4 ,25,15,50)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2, pe1.GetLeft + 14, 10 , 45 , "FOLYO")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)

	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME

	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	sayfaBoyu = 100
	sayfaEni = 150
	Ustmargin = 5
	AltMargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 100
	EtiketEni = 150
	solMargin = 5
	sagMargin = 5
	satiryukseklik = 5
	xSatirAra = 7
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)

	Ust = UstMargin+60

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		Ust = UstMargin
		if EtiketNo <> 1 then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next

	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		'res = pe1.Show()
msgbox "xxx"
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Call SabitBasliklar(Pe1)

	FirmaKodu = "1049"
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	OperatorKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)

	xSol= 25	
	Gen1= 48

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	'Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+55, SolMargin+10, 15, 50, "EAN128", Trim(BarkodNo), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 22)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+70, xSol, 2.5*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik+5, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(FirmaKodu))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& "TEKKAN PLASTÝK")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(OperatorKodu))
	'Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "&Doc.ChangeNumberStyleCD( Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2)))


	Call SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)

End Sub

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function


Sub SabitBasliklar(Pe1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO2"))	
	res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin-2 ,SolMargin+20, 40,40,logo,0)
	Res = Pe1.pensize(0.3)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Gen1 = 24
	Ust = UstMargin+12	
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+5, 2*SatirYukseklik, 70, "ARÇELÝK A.Þ.")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Eskiþehir Kompresör Ýþl.")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Malzeme Tanýtým Kartý")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Firma Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Irsaliye Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Üretim Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Operatör Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Miktar")
End Sub

Sub SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)

	KutuEn= 17 
	KutuBoy = 25
	KutuUst= UstMargin+30
	KutuSol= 80
	Ara = 5


	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(90)
	' ------ Reach / Pah kutusu ------
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "REACH / PACH")
	' ------ Rohs kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "ROHS")
	' ------ Gkk kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "GKK")
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)	
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(270)
	' ------ Dikey Barkodlar -----
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin, 15, 30, "CODE128", Trim(StokKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, "CODE128", Trim(FirmaKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
End Sub
Sub Malzemeturu()
	ACM = "120 01 003"
	ALG = "120 01 004"
	AKI = "120 01 054"
	TUMT = "120 01 022"

	ACM = Doc.LeftJustify(ACM,16)
	ALG = Doc.LeftJustify(ALG,16)
	AKI = Doc.LeftJustify(AKI,16)
	TUMT = Doc.LeftJustify(TUMT,16)
	YanlisCari = 0
	sorunlustok = ""
STOK20T.SAFEPACK
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2G("fj0kdgh","STOK20T","","","KOD",-1)
	if S20T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to S20T.GetRecCount()

			Set SGT = Doc.Select1("fjkdgh","STOK0GT","STOKCODE = '"& S20T.KOD(kk) &"' & KOD = '"& AKI &"'","",-1)
				if SGT.GetRecCount() = 0 then
					sorunlustok = sorunlustok & S20T.KOD(kk)
					YanlisCari = 1
					Exit Sub
				End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
	YanlisCari = 0

	Call malzemeturu()

	'if YanlisCari = 1 then
	'	y = doc.d7MsgBox(sorunlustok&" Kodlu ürün bu etiket formu ile basýlamaz!...","Freedom",MB_OK)
	'	Exit Sub
	'End if


	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	sayfaBoyu = 100
	sayfaEni = 150
	Ustmargin = 5
	AltMargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 100
	EtiketEni = 150
	solMargin = 5
	sagMargin = 5
	satiryukseklik = 5
	xSatirAra = 7
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)

	Ust = UstMargin+60

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		Ust = UstMargin
		if EtiketNo <> 1 then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next

	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Call SabitBasliklar(Pe1)

	FirmaKodu = "1049"
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	EvrakNo= d7LB.EVRAKNO(satirNo)
	LotNo = d7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	xSol= 25	
	Gen1= 48

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	'Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+55, SolMargin+10, 15, 50, "EAN128", Trim(BarkodNo), "")

'	res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin+15 ,SolMargin + 23, 20,40,logo,114)
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+3, SolMargin+6, 12, 50, "EAN128", Trim(StokKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+3, SolMargin+65, 12, 20, "EAN128", Trim(tarih), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, xSol, 2*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik*2, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+2*satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(EvrakNo))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(LotNo))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
'	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "&Doc.ChangeNumberStyleCD( Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2)))

	Call SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)

End Sub

Sub SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)

	KutuEn= 17 
	KutuBoy = 25
	KutuUst= UstMargin+30
	KutuSol= 80
	Ara = 5


	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(90)
	' ------ Reach / Pah kutusu ------
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "KABUL")
	' ------ Rohs kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "RED")
	' ------ Gkk kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "")
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)	
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(270)
	' ------ Dikey Barkodlar -----
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin, 15, 30, "CODE128", Trim(StokKodu), "")
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, "CODE128", Trim(FirmaKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
End Sub
Sub Malzemeturu()
	ACM = "120 01 003"
	ALG = "120 01 004"
	AKI = "120 01 054"
	TUMT = "120 01 022"

	ACM = Doc.LeftJustify(ACM,16)
	ALG = Doc.LeftJustify(ALG,16)
	AKI = Doc.LeftJustify(AKI,16)
	TUMT = Doc.LeftJustify(TUMT,16)
	YanlisCari = 0
	sorunlustok = ""
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2G("fj0kdgh","STOK20T","","","KOD",-1)
	if S20T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to S20T.GetRecCount()
			Set SGT = Doc.Select1("fjkdgh","STOK0GT","STOKCODE = '"& S20T.KOD(kk) &"'","",-1)
				if SGT.GetRecCount() > 0 then
					for ii = 1 to SGT.GetRecCount()
						if ACM = SGT.KOD(ii) or ALG = SGT.KOD(ii) or AKI = SGT.KOD(ii) or TUMT = SGT.KOD(ii)Then
							sorunlustok = sorunlustok & S20T.KOD(kk)
							YanlisCari = 1
							Exit Sub
						End if
					Next
				End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 45
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10 
	SolMargin = 20
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5.5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 52
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 20 * satirYuksekligi
	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satinalma Siparisi",  SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni,"")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = PERSB08T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	ssklogo = Doc.Getd7Directory_SwRoot & "wd\images\ssklogo.bmp"
	res = pe1.picture(10,solmargin+25,16,16,ssklogo,16)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("TimesNewRoman",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("Left")
	res = pe1.write(29, Solmargin + 13, satiryuksekligi, 50 , "SOSYAL SÝGORTALAR KURUMU")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom-1, Solmargin + 8, satiryuksekligi, 100 , "SÝGORTALI EKSÝK GÜN BÝLDÝRÝM FORMU")
	res = pe1.box(20, solmargin + 130, satiryuksekligi * 4,50)		
	res = pe1.line(20 + satiryuksekligi, solmargin + 130, 0,50)
	res = pe1.line(20 + satiryuksekligi * 2, solmargin + 143, 0,37	)	
	res = pe1.line(20 + satiryuksekligi * 3, solmargin + 130, 0,50)		

	res = pe1.line(20 + satiryuksekligi, solmargin + 143, satiryuksekligi * 2,0)		
	res = pe1.line(20 + satiryuksekligi, solmargin + 159, satiryuksekligi * 3,0)

	res = pe1.write(16, sagmargin-10, satiryuksekligi,10,"EK:8")		
	res = pe1.write(21, solmargin + 145, satiryuksekligi,15,"BELGENÝN")		
	res = pe1.write(22 + satiryuksekligi, solmargin + 131, satiryuksekligi,10,"Ait")		
	res = pe1.write(20 + satiryuksekligi * 2, solmargin + 131, satiryuksekligi,10,"Olduðu")		
	res = pe1.write(21 + satiryuksekligi, solmargin + 145, satiryuksekligi,10,"Yýl")		
	res = pe1.write(21 + satiryuksekligi * 2, solmargin + 145, satiryuksekligi,10,"Ay")		
	res = pe1.write(21 + satiryuksekligi * 3, solmargin + 131, satiryuksekligi,30,"Sayfa No")

	Yil = PERSB08E.YIL(1)
	Ay = PERSB08E.AY(1)
	Sayfa = pe1.GetCurrentPage()
	res = pe1.write(21 + satiryuksekligi, solmargin + 165, satiryuksekligi,10,Yil)
	res = pe1.write(21 + satiryuksekligi * 2, solmargin + 167, satiryuksekligi,10,Ay)		
	res = pe1.write(21 + satiryuksekligi * 3, solmargin + 168, satiryuksekligi,10,Sayfa)		
 	
	res = pe1.box(ustmargin, solmargin, satiryuksekligi * 6,sagmargin - solmargin)
	'yatay izgiler		
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 1.5, solmargin, 0,sagmargin - solmargin)		
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 8, solmargin, 0,sagmargin - solmargin - 8)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 20, solmargin, 0,sagmargin - solmargin)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 19, solmargin + 65, 0,107)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 14.5, solmargin + 65, 0,107)
	'dikey izgiler
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 20, solmargin + 24, 13, 0)		
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 1.5, solmargin + 61,18.5 ,0)		
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 8, solmargin + 65,11, 0)		
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 8, solmargin + 172,11, 0)		

	res = pe1.line(ustmargin + 8, solmargin + 70,11, 0)

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	If trim(PERSB08T.PERSONELSICILNO(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 52,10,10,10,"")
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
			If sirano > 19 Then
				'Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
				siraNo = 1
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
				Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
				satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			End if
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		res = pe1.selectfont("Tahoma", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 3
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = trim(cstr(PERSB08T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,satirNo)))
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_POS"  Then
			   Degisken = satirNo
			Else 
				Degisken = ""
			End If
		End If
		If DegiskenAdi = "PEGK" then
			res = pe1.setalign("right")
			degisken = doc.NumFormat(degisken,10,2)
		End if
		If DegiskenAdi = "PEGS" then
			sol = solmargin + 2
			res = pe1.setalign("Center")
			degisken = doc.NumFormat(degisken,10,0)
		End if
		If DegiskenAdi = "_NEDEN" then
			Abc = PERSB08T.EGBN(satirno)
			Degisken = doc.GetGecoValue("|BORSSKEG","AD",Abc,-1)
			degisken = trim(degisken)
		End if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 2
			ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
		End If
	Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "SIGORTASICILNO"
	Grid(2,0) = "AD_SOYAD"
	Grid(3,0) = "PEGK"
	Grid(4,0) = "EBGS"
	Grid(5,0) = "EGBN"
	Grid(6,0) = "_NEDEN"
	Grid(7,0) = "DEGERLENDIRMESONUCU"

	Grid(0,1) = 12
	Grid(1,1) = 29
	Grid(2,1) = 39
	Grid(3,1) = 27
	Grid(4,1) = 19
	Grid(5,1) = 8
	Grid(6,1) = 25
	Grid(7,1) = 20

	Grid(0,4) = "N"
	Grid(1,4) = "N"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"

End Sub

Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, AltMargin, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketEni, d7LB, satiryukseklik
Public xSatirAra,YanlisCari,sorunlustok

'Sub PrintForm()

Sub PrintLabel()
	YanlisCari = 0

	Call malzemeturu()

	'if YanlisCari = 1 then
	'	y = doc.d7MsgBox(sorunlustok&" Kodlu ürün bu etiket formu ile basýlamaz!...","Freedom",MB_OK)
	'	Exit Sub
	'End if


	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	sayfaBoyu = 100
	sayfaEni = 150
	Ustmargin = 5
	AltMargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 100
	EtiketEni = 150
	solMargin = 5
	sagMargin = 5
	satiryukseklik = 5
	xSatirAra = 7
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)

	Ust = UstMargin+60

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		Ust = UstMargin
		if EtiketNo <> 1 then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
		end if
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar)
	Next

	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Call SabitBasliklar(Pe1)

	FirmaKodu = "1049"
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	EvrakNo= d7LB.EVRAKNO(satirNo)
	LotNo = d7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	OperatorKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)
	HmKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT02(satirNo)
	Saat = Doc.Saat(5)

	xSol= 25	
	Gen1= 48

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	'Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin+55, SolMargin+10, 15, 50, "EAN128", Trim(BarkodNo), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, xSol, 2*satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokKodu))
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik*2, Gen1, ": "& Trim(StokAdi))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+2*satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(EvrakNo))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(LotNo))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih))&" - "&Saat)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ":")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(OperatorKodu))
'	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "&Doc.ChangeNumberStyleCD( Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Miktar,20,2)))
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1, ": "& Trim(HmKodu))

	Call SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)

End Sub

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function


Sub SabitBasliklar(Pe1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))	
	res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin+15 ,SolMargin + 23, 20,40,logo,114)
	Res = Pe1.pensize(0.3)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	Gen1 = 24
	Ust = UstMargin+12	
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+5, 2*SatirYukseklik, 70, "T E K K A N.")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+SatirYukseklik, SolMargin, SatirYukseklik, 80, "Malzeme Tanýtým Kartý")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
	Res = Pe1.Write(UstMargin+75, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Malzeme Adý")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+2*satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Üretim Fiþ No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Lot No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Üretim Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Vardiya No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Operator Kodu")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Miktar")
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "Hammadde Lot No")
End Sub

Sub SagBlokKutularDikeyBarkodlar(Pe1, StokKodu, FirmaKodu)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)

	KutuEn= 17 
	KutuBoy = 25
	KutuUst= UstMargin+30
	KutuSol= 80
	Ara = 5


	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(90)
	' ------ Reach / Pah kutusu ------
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "KABUL")
	' ------ Rohs kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "RED")
	' ------ Gkk kutusu ------
	KutuUst = KutuUst+ KutuBoy + 5
	Res = Pe1.WriteBox(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuBoy, KutuEn, "")
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)	
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(270)
	' ------ Dikey Barkodlar -----
	Res = Pe1.NoPen()
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin, 15, 30, "CODE128", Trim(StokKodu), "")
'	Res = Pe1.Barcode(UstMargin, SolMargin+75, 15, 20, "CODE128", Trim(FirmaKodu), "")
	Res = Pe1.Pensize(0.4)
	Res = Pe1.SetRotation(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
End Sub
Sub Malzemeturu()
	ACM = "120 01 003"
	ALG = "120 01 004"
	AKI = "120 01 054"
	TUMT = "120 01 022"

	ACM = Doc.LeftJustify(ACM,16)
	ALG = Doc.LeftJustify(ALG,16)
	AKI = Doc.LeftJustify(AKI,16)
	TUMT = Doc.LeftJustify(TUMT,16)
	YanlisCari = 0
	sorunlustok = ""
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2G("fj0kdgh","STOK20T","","","KOD",-1)
	if S20T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		for kk = 1 to S20T.GetRecCount()
			Set SGT = Doc.Select1("fjkdgh","STOK0GT","STOKCODE = '"& S20T.KOD(kk) &"'","",-1)
				if SGT.GetRecCount() > 0 then
					for ii = 1 to SGT.GetRecCount()
						if ACM = SGT.KOD(ii) or ALG = SGT.KOD(ii) or AKI = SGT.KOD(ii) or TUMT = SGT.KOD(ii)Then
							sorunlustok = sorunlustok & S20T.KOD(kk)
							YanlisCari = 1
							Exit Sub
						End if
					Next
				End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari)

	FirmaKodu = "1049"
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo))

	birim = d7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo)

	LotNo = d7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	OperatorKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)

	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop-satiryukseklik, Pe1.GetRight, satiryukseklik, 15, "BÝRÝM")
	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Black", 18)

	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)

	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Black", 22)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop, Pe1.GetRight, satiryukseklik*2, 110,  Trim(StokKodu))

	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)

	Res = Pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Black", 16)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop, Pe1.GetRight, satiryukseklik*2, 30,  Trim(EvrakNo))

'	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "LOT NO")
'	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetTop+satiryukseklik, xSol, satiryukseklik, Gen1,  Trim(LotNo))

	Res = Pe1.SetBold(0)
	Res = Pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(Pe1.GetBottom, SolMargin, satiryukseklik, Gen1, "ÝÞ EMRÝ NO")
